[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU),
module name `tpu`, implementing the EXACT per-cycle behavior of the logikbench
`tpu` benchmark (zeroasiccorp/logikbench, MIT).

FUNCTION: computes C = A * B tile products on an 8x8 grid of processing
elements. B (the "weight" matrix, 8-bit signed) is loaded into the array and
held; A (the "activation" matrix, 8-bit signed) streams through; result rows
C (32-bit signed) drain out the bottom.

PROTOCOL:
  1. Weight load: assert w_valid for 8 cycles; w_data carries one weight row
     per cycle, 8 signed bytes packed { col 7 ... col 0 }, BOTTOM matrix row
     first (cycle k carries B[7-k][*]; the weights shift south). After 8
     cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
  2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle (packed the
     same way). Row m of the result, C[m][*] packed { col 7 ... col 0 } as 8
     signed 32-bit words, appears on c_data with c_valid high exactly 15
     (= 2*8-1) compute cycles after its A row was accepted.
  - The array advances only while w_valid is low: asserting w_valid stalls
    any in-flight computation (and reloads weights). Zeros are injected while
    a_valid is low, so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
  - Multiple A tiles may stream back-to-back against the same loaded weights.

CYCLE ACCURACY: c_valid is compared against the original design EVERY cycle;
c_data is compared on every cycle where c_valid is high. The 15-cycle
compute-latency and the stall semantics are part of the contract.

Ports:
  clk, rst (async active-high reset, asserted only before the stream begins)
  w_valid, w_data[63:0]
  a_valid, a_data[63:0]
  c_valid, c_data[255:0]

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` is the
original logikbench RTL (tpu_pe / tpu_array / tpu: PE mesh, per-row input
skew, per-column output de-skew, valid pipeline). It passes every cycle of the golden
trace in `vectors.dat` (directed zero/identity/extreme tiles, random tiles,
back-to-back streams, a_valid gaps, mid-drain weight reloads, random soak).
Study it, then optimize while preserving the cycle-accurate behavior. The
sibling benchmark `tpu_spire` shares the identical testbench and vectors
with a SpireHDL starting point.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Processing element (PE) for the weight-stationary systolic array (TPU MXU).
//
// Each PE holds one stationary signed weight 'w'. Two independent datapaths
// share the cell:
//
//   Weight load (ld): the weights form a per-column shift register that loads
//   from the top. On each ld cycle the PE captures the weight from the PE above
//   (w_north) and presents its stored weight downward (w_south). After N load
//   cycles every PE in a column holds its final stationary weight.
//
//   Compute (en): the activation flows west to east (registered, one hop per
//   cycle) and the partial sum flows north to south, accumulating the local
//   product a_west * w. This is the only arithmetic cell in the array: one
//   signed DW x DW multiply plus an ACCW-bit add.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)
   (
    input		  clk,
    input		  rst,	      // synchronous, active high
    input		  ld,	      // weight-load shift enable
    input		  en,	      // compute enable
    input [DW-1:0]	  w_north,    // weight in from PE above
    input [DW-1:0]	  a_west,     // activation in from west
    input [ACCW-1:0]	  psum_north, // partial sum in from above
    output [DW-1:0]	  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below
    output reg [DW-1:0]	  a_east,     // activation out to east
    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below
    );

   // stationary weight register; presented downward for the load shift chain
   reg [DW-1:0] w;
   assign w_south = w;

   // local product: the ONLY signed operation. Operands are interpreted as
   // signed here via $signed; the 2*DW-bit result holds the two's-complement
   // product, which is then sign-extended to the accumulator width below.
   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);

   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst) begin
         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};
         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};
         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
      end
      else begin
         if (ld)
           w <= w_north;
         if (en) begin
            a_east     <= a_west;
            psum_south <= psum_north +
                          {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};
         end
      end
   end

endmodule
//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).
//
// One tpu_pe per grid element, wired as a 2D mesh (per CLAUDE.md: replicated
// hardware via generate, never a procedural unroll):
//
//   - weights load from the top: w_top[j] feeds column j; each column is a
//     vertical shift register (w_south of PE(i,j) -> w_north of PE(i+1,j)).
//   - activations flow west to east: a_left[i] enters row i; a_east of PE(i,j)
//     feeds a_west of PE(i,j+1).
//   - partial sums flow north to south and accumulate; the top row's psum_north
//     is tied to zero (fresh accumulation), and c_bot[j] is the column-j result
//     leaving the bottom row.
//
// With weight B[i][j] held at PE(i,j) and activation A[m][i] entering row i,
// column j computes C[m][j] = sum_i A[m][i] * B[i][j]. Input/output skew (which
// time-aligns rows and columns) lives in the tpu top, not here.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu_array
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N PEs)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	// synchronous, active high
    input		ld,	// weight-load shift enable
    input		en,	// compute enable
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_top,	// weight inputs, one per column (load)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_left,	// activation inputs, one per row
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_bot	// column results, one per column
    );

   // mesh nets: a_h carries activations east, psum_v carries sums south,
   // w_v carries weights south during load.
   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];
   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];
   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];

   genvar	   i, j;
   generate
      // west edge: activation inputs into column 0 of each row
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west
         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];
      end
      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out

... (showing lines 1-118 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[Step 2/60]
// Self-checking testbench for the tpu benchmark (8x8 weight-stationary
// systolic matmul tile). Reads vectors.dat, one line per clock cycle:
//   w_valid w_data(64b) a_valid a_data(64b)   c_valid c_data(256b)   (hex)
// The expected outputs are the ORIGINAL logikbench tpu's per-cycle behavior;
// c_valid and c_data are compared CYCLE-ACCURATELY every cycle (c_data only
// while c_valid is high — the original holds stale data between results).
module tb;
  localparam N = 8, DW = 8, ACCW = 32;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic clk, rst;
  logic w_valid, a_valid, c_valid;
  logic [N*DW-1:0] w_data, a_data;
  logic [N*ACCW-1:0] c_data;

  tpu dut
    (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .w_valid(w_valid), .w_data(w_data),
     .a_valid(a_valid), .a_data(a_data), .c_valid(c_valid), .c_data(c_data));

  initial clk = 0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  integer fd, rc, line_num;
  string line_buf;
  logic s_wv, s_av, e_cv;
  logic [63:0] s_wd, s_ad;
  logic [255:0] e_cd;

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    w_valid = 0; a_valid = 0; w_data = 0; a_data = 0;
    rst = 1;
    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;

    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    line_num = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      line_num = line_num + 1;
      void'($fgets(line_buf, fd));
      if (line_buf.len() == 0) continue;
      if (line_buf.substr(0, 0) == "#") continue;
      rc = $sscanf(line_buf, "%h %h %h %h %h %h", s_wv, s_wd, s_av, s_ad, e_cv, e_cd);
      if (rc != 6) continue;
      w_valid = s_wv; w_data = s_wd; a_valid = s_av; a_data = s_ad;
      @(negedge clk);
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      if (c_valid !== e_cv || (e_cv && c_data !== e_cd)) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR line=%0d exp(cv=%b cd=%h) got(cv=%b cd=%h)",
                 line_num, e_cv, e_cd, c_valid, c_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
        if (total_errors > 20) begin
          $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
          $fatal(1, "FAIL (too many errors, aborting)");
        end
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

      // top edge: zero partial sums, weight inputs; bottom edge: results out
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge
         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};
         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];

//#############################################################################
// Copyright: Zero ASIC. All rights Reserved.
// Author: Andreas Olofsson
// License:  MIT (see LICENSE file in LogikBench repository)
//#############################################################################
//
// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).
//
// Computes one tile product C = A * B, where A and B are N x N signed operands
// (DW bits) and C is N x N signed (ACCW bits). B is the "weight" matrix, loaded
// into and held by the array; A is the "activation" matrix, streamed through; C
// drains out the bottom. Built from a tpu_array of N x N tpu_pe cells.
//
// Operation has two phases the host drives via valids:
//
//   1. Weight load: assert w_valid for N cycles. w_data carries one weight row
//      per cycle, N signed weights packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }. Because the
//      array loads top-down (shift south), push the BOTTOM matrix row first:
//      cycle k carries B[N-1-k][*]. After N cycles PE(i,j) holds B[i][j].
//
//   2. Compute: assert a_valid while streaming A one row per cycle, a_data
//      packed { col N-1 ... col 0 } = A[m][N-1..0]. Row m of the result, C[m][*]
//      (packed the same way), appears on c_data with c_valid high, 2*N-1 cycles
//      after its A row was accepted.
//
// Time alignment: row i of A is delayed i cycles entering the array (input
// skew) so partial sums chain correctly down each column; column j of the
// result is delayed N-1-j cycles (output de-skew) so a full result row leaves
// together. Both skew networks and the valid pipeline advance only with the
// array (compute enable), so the 2*N-1 latency is exact. Per CLAUDE.md the skew
// chains are generated per-row/column shift registers, not a procedural unroll.
//
//#############################################################################

module tpu
  #(parameter N = 8,	 // array dimension (N x N)
    parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)
   (
    input		clk,
    input		rst,	 // synchronous, active high
    input		w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	w_data,	 // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    input		a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)
    input [N*DW-1:0]	a_data,	 // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    output		c_valid, // result-row valid
    output [N*ACCW-1:0]	c_data	 // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }
    );

   // total injection-to-output latency, in compute cycles
   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;

   // load weights while w_valid; otherwise the array computes (and drains)
   wire	      ld = w_valid;
   wire	      en = ~w_valid;

   //##########################################################################
   // Input skew: row i of the activation is delayed i cycles. Zero is injected
   // when a_valid is low so idle/drain cycles add nothing to the accumulators.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;
   genvar	   i;
   generate
      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew
         wire [DW-1:0] din =
		       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};
         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;
         end
         else begin : g_skewn
            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  sr[0] <= din;
                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)
                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // The systolic array.
   //##########################################################################
   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;
   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array
     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),
      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));

   //##########################################################################
   // Output de-skew: column j is delayed N-1-j cycles so all columns of a
   // result row line up. Column N-1 (longest path) needs no delay.
   //##########################################################################
   genvar j;
   generate
      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew
         localparam D = N - 1 - j;
         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];
         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;
         end
         else begin : g_deskewn
            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];
            integer k;
            always @(posedge clk) begin
               if (rst) begin
                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};
               end
               else if (en) begin
                  dr[0] <= cin;
                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)
                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];
               end
            end
            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];
         end
      end
   endgenerate

   //##########################################################################
   // Valid pipeline: c_valid is a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.
   //##########################################################################
   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;
   always @(posedge clk) begin
      if (rst)
        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};
      else if (en)
        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};
   end
   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];

endmodule

... (showing lines 118-282 of 282; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
I've checked the starting point and testbench. The plan is as follows:

1. **v0**: Baseline — copy of starting_point.v, evaluate and obtain reference cost.
2. **v1**: Reduce the internal bit-width of the partial sum (the internal psum is 19 bits instead of 32 bits: `|sum| ≤ 8·2^14 = 2^17`, so 19-bit signed is sufficient, then sign-extend at the output); similarly shrink the deskew register.
3. **v2**: Remove the reset from the data path (Verilator zero-initializes; reset is only asserted before the stream starts).
4. Further structural experimentation (CSA, per-row bit widths) and tuning of target_delay.

Key invariant: `c_valid` matches bit-accurately every cycle, and `c_data` matches during valid cycles — latency is 15 compute cycles, with stall semantics via the `en = ~w_valid` enable.

I'll create the v0 baseline and the v1 optimization (as separate files):

  -> create_file(filename='design_v1.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v1:\n//  - Internal partial sums carried in PW=19 bits instead of 32.\n//    Proof of sufficiency: each product |a*b| <= 128*128 = 2^14; any internal\n//    psum is the sum of <= 8 products of ONE A row (the top edge injects 0\n//    every cycle, so accumulation never spans more than 8 products), hence\n//    |psum| <= 8 * 2^14 = 2^17, which fits signed 19 bits (max magnitude\n//    representable 2^18). [ASSISTANT]
v1 passed with cost **3,235,640** (-30% vs baseline). The critical path is multiplier + 19-bit accumulator inside the PE. Next: v2 — remove resets from the data-path registers (Verilator zero-initializes; since weights are always loaded before use, a reset isn't needed for the stored values. This eliminates reset muxes from ~3000 flops):


  -> create_file(filename='design_v2.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v2:\n//  - PW=19-bit internal partial sums (proven sufficient: any internal psum is\n//    the sum of <= 8 products of one A row, |psum| <= 8*2^14 = 2^17).\n//  - No resets on datapath registers (weights, activations, psums, skew,\n//    deskew). The array is always fully loaded (weights) or flushed with\n//    zeros (computes propagate zeros when a_valid is low) before results are\n//    observed, so reset values are never observed. Simulation starts with\n//    registers at zero anyway. Only the 15-bit valid pipeline keeps a reset.\n// Cycle-accurate behavior identical to the original.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // internal partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east     <= a_west;\n         psum_south <= psum_north +\n                       {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // internal partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // internal partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with narrow (PW-bit) partial sums.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design_v3.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v3:\n//  - PW=19-bit internal partial sums (any internal psum is the sum of <= 8\n//    products of one A row, |psum| <= 8*2^14 = 2^17, fits signed 19 bits).\n//  - Datapath registers have no reset (values never observed before being\n//    properly loaded/flushed; sim starts at zero).\n//  - Sign-magnitude MAC: prod = s ? ~mag + 1 : mag (s = sign of product).\n//    psum + prod = psum + s + (s? ~mag : mag). Since |psum| <= 2^17, psum+s\n//    cannot overflow 18 bits, so bits [18:16] of the result are\n//    psum[18:16] ^ s (plain XOR), and only bits [15:0] need a carry chain.\n//    The final 3 bits are tiny XOR/AND logic off the psum register, and the\n//    accumulator add shrinks from 19 to 16 bits.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // internal partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   localparam LB = 16;  // low part width; ACCW-LB high bits are XOR-only\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // signed product via sign-magnitude operands\n   wire s   = a_west[DW-1] ^ w[DW-1]; // product sign\n   wire [DW-1:0] am = a_west[DW-1] ? (~a_west + 1'b1) : a_west;\n   wire [DW-1:0] wm = w[DW-1]    ? (~w    + 1'b1) : w;\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] mag = am * wm;           // unsigned magnitude product\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] addend = s ? ~mag : mag; // two's complement w/o final +1\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum = psum_north;\n   // low part: full ripple/CLA through bit LB-1, plus the sign bit as cin\n   wire [LB:0] lo = psum[LB-1:0] + addend[LB-1:0] + s;\n   // high part: bits [ACCW-2:LB] flip when s=1; MSB also gets the carry-out\n   wire [ACCW-LB-2:0] hi_mid = psum[ACCW-2:LB] ^ {(ACCW-LB-1){s}};\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] nxt = {lo[LB] ^ s, hi_mid, lo[LB-1:0]};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east     <= a_west;\n         psum_south <= nxt;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 19) // internal partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // internal partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with narrow (PW-bit) partial sums.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
Now, let me fix the bug in v3 (the XOR trick for the upper bits is mathematically incorrect when there is carry-in) — but actually, first let's try a cleaner idea, **v4: v2 + an explicit Kogge-Stone prefix adder for the accumulation**. This is because the tail end of the critical path is a 19-bit accumulator adder. It's unclear whether Yosys's default adder mapping is optimal:


  -> create_file(filename='design_v4.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v4:\n//  v2 base (PW=19 psums, no datapath resets) + the PE accumulation adder\n//  written as an explicit Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix adder (5 levels for\n//  19 bits) instead of relying on the tool's default ripple structure.\n\nmodule ksadd19 (\n    input  [18:0] A,\n    input  [18:0] B,\n    output [18:0] S\n);\n P0[k-1];\n      end\n      // level 2: offset 2\n      wire [18:0] G2, P2;\n      assign G2[1:0] = G1[1:0];  assign P2[1:0] = P1[1:0];\n      for (k = 2; k < 19; k = k + 1) begin : gl2\n         assign G2[k] = G1[k] | (P1[k] & G1[k-2]);\n         assign P2[k] = P1[k] & P1[k-2];\n      end\n      // level 3: offset 4\n      wire [18:0] G3, P3;\n      assign G3[3:0] = G2[3:0];  assign P3[3:0] = psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_x = [ASSISTANT]
v4 passes at **3,197,310**. Next is v5: **per-row partial-sum width tapering** — row *i* holds at most i+1 products (|sum| ≤ (i+1)·2¹⁴), so rows 0, 1-2, 3-6, 7 can carry 16/17/18/19 bits respectively. This narrows about 90% of the accumulator adders and psum registers:


  -> create_file(filename='design_v5.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v5:\n//  - Per-row tapered partial-sum widths. Row i accumulates at most i+1\n//    products (the top edge injects 0 every cycle), and each product has\n//    magnitude <= 2^14, so |psum at row i| <= (i+1)*2^14. Signed PW bits\n//    suffice when (i+1)*2^14 <= 2^(PW-2):\n//      row 0      -> 16 bits (1 product,  max 16384  <= 16384)\n//      rows 1-2   -> 17 bits (<=3 products, max 49152 <= 65536)\n//      rows 3-6   -> 18 bits (<=7 products, max 114688<= 131072)\n//      row 7      -> 19 bits (8 products,  max 131072 <= 131072)\n//    Widths are non-decreasing going south, so boundaries connect bit-for-bit.\n//  - Generic Kogge-Stone prefix adders for the accumulation.\n//  - No datapath resets (sim starts zero; values always loaded/flushed before\n//    observation). Only vpipe keeps a reset.\n\nmodule ksadd #(parameter W = 19) (\n    input  [W-1:0] A,\n    input  [W-1:0] B,\n    output [W-1:0] S\n);\n   wire [W-1:0] G [0:5];\n   wire [W-1:0] P [0:5];\n   assign G[0] = A & B;\n   assign P[0] = A ^ B;\n   genvar l, k;\n   generate\n      for (l = 1; l <= 5; l = l + 1) begin : lvl\n         for (k = 0; k < W; k = k + 1) begin : bit\n            if (k >= (1 << l)) begin : merge\n               assign G[l][k] = G[l-1][k] | (P[l-1][k] & G[l-1][k-(1<<l)]);\n               assign P[l][k] = P[l-1][k] & P[l-1][k-(1<<l)];\n            end\n            else begin : pass\n               assign G[l][k] = G[l-1][k];\n               assign P[l][k] = P[l-1][k];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign S = P[0] ^ {G[5][W-2:0], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // this PE's partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [PW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [PW-1:0]\t  psum_south  // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [PW-1:0] prod_x = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] nxt;\n   ksadd #(.W(PW)) u_add (.A(psum_north), .B(prod_x), .S(nxt));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east     <= a_west;\n         psum_south <= nxt;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8)\t // operand width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [151:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column (19b each)\n    );\n\n   // tapered widths per row\n   function integer pwf;\n      input integer i;\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            0: pwf = 16;\n            1,2: pwf = 17;\n            3,4,5,6: pwf = 18;\n            default: pwf = 19;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [18:0]     psum_v [0:N][0:N-1]; // extra top bits are unused (0)\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = 19'd0;\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*19 +: 19] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .PW(pwf(i))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j][pwf(i)-1:0]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j][pwf(i)-1:0]));\n         end\n      end\n      // tie unused upper bits of each psum net to zero\n      for (i = 1; i <= N; i = i + 1) begin : g_tie\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tiec\n            if (pwf(i-1) < 19) begin : tiehi\n               assign psum_v[i][j][18:pwf(i-1)] = {19-pwf(i-1){1'b0}};\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // output partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with tapered partial sums, 19-bit output.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[Step 18/60]
// Optimized TPU MXU, v5:
//  - Per-row tapered partial-sum widths. Row i accumulates at most i+1
//    products (the top edge injects 0 every cycle), and each product has
//    magnitude <= 2^14, so |psum at row i| <= (i+1)*2^14. Signed PW bits
//    suffice when (i+1)*2^14 <= 2^(PW-2):
//      row 0      -> 16 bits (1 product,  max 16384  <= 16384)
//      rows 1-2   -> 17 bits (<=3 products, max 49152 <= 65536)
//      rows 3-6   -> 18 bits (<=7 products, max 114688<= 131072)
//      row 7      -> 19 bits (8 products,  max 131072 <= 131072)
//    Widths are non-decreasing going south, so boundaries connect bit-for-bit.
//  - Generic Kogge-Stone prefix adders for the accumulation.
//  - No datapath resets (sim starts zero; values always loaded/flushed before
//    observation). Only vpipe keeps a reset.

module ksadd #(parameter W = 19) (
    input  [W-1:0] A,
    input  [W-1:0] B,
    output [W-1:0] S
);
   // flattened levels: G[l*W + k], P[l*W + k], levels 0..5
   wire [6*W-1:0] G, P;
   assign G[W-1:0] = A & B;
   assign P[W-1:0] = A ^ B;
   genvar l, k;
   generate
      for (l = 1; l <= 5; l = l + 1) begin : lvl
         for (k = 0; k < W; k = k + 1) begin : bit
            if (k >= (1 << l)) begin : merge
               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k] |
                                 (P[(l-1)*W+k] & G[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<l)]);
               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k] & P[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<l)];
            end
            else begin : pass
               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k];
               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k];
            end
         end
      end
   endgenerate
   assign S = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[5*W + W-2 : 5*W], 1'b0};
endmodule

module tpu_pe
  #(parameter DW = 8,	 // operand width (signed)
    parameter PW = 19)	 // this PE's partial-sum width (signed)

... (showing lines 1-45 of 231; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
The tapering logic is correct, but I found the real bug: in `g_tie` I tie the unused upper bits of each psum net to **zero**, when they must be **sign-extensions** of the MSB of the tapered width. Negative partial sums lose their sign that way. Let me fix it:


  -> edit_file(filename='design_v5.v', old_string="      // tie unused upper bits of each psum net to zero\n      for (i = 1; i <= N; i = i + 1) begin : g_tie\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tiec\n            if (pwf(i-1) < 19) begin : tiehi\n               assign psum_v[i][j][18:pwf(i-1)] = {19-pwf(i-1){1'b0}};\n            end\n         end\n      end", new_string='      // sign-extend the unused upper bits of each psum net\n      for (i = 1; i <= N; i = i + 1) begin : g_tie\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_tiec\n            if (pwf(i-1) < 19) begin : tiehi\n               assign psum_v[i][j][18:pwf(i-1)] =\n                      {19-pwf(i-1){psum_v[i][j][pwf(i-1)-1]}};\n            end\n         end\n      end')

[ASSISTANT]
The tapered design has a multi-driver slice issue. Let me rebuild with a clean structure where each PE outputs a fully sign-extended 19-bit value (narrow internal register + adder, combinational sign-extension at the output port). This eliminates all partial-slice driving. Let me create v6:


  -> create_file(filename='design_v6.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v6:\n//  - Per-row tapered partial-sum widths. Row i accumulates at most i+1\n//    products, each magnitude <= 2^14, so |psum at row i| <= (i+1)*2^14.\n//    Minimum signed widths (2^(PW-1)-1 >= max magnitude):\n//      row 0 -> 16, row 1 -> 17, row 2 -> 17, row 3 -> 18,\n//      rows 4-6 -> 18, row 7 -> 19\n//  - Each PE keeps a NARROW PW-bit accumulator register and uses a NARROW\n//    PW-bit Kogge-Stone adder, but presents a full AW=19-bit sign-extended\n//    value on its output port (pure wiring). This keeps every inter-row net a\n//    single-driver 19-bit bus: no partial-slice drives, no tie logic.\n//  - No datapath resets; only the valid pipeline keeps a reset.\n\nmodule ksadd #(parameter W = 19) (\n    input  [W-1:0] A,\n    input  [W-1:0] B,\n    output [W-1:0] S\n);\n   wire [6*W-1:0] G, P;\n   assign G[W-1:0] = A & B;\n   assign P[W-1:0] = A ^ B;\n   genvar l, k;\n   generate\n      for (l = 1; l <= 5; l = l + 1) begin : lvl\n         for (k = 0; k < W; k = k + 1) begin : b\n            if (k >= (1 << l)) begin : merge\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k] |\n                                 (P[(l-1)*W+k] & G[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<l)]);\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k] & P[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<l)];\n            end\n            else begin : pass\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k];\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign S = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[5*W + W-2 : 5*W], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19,\t // inter-row (port) partial-sum width\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // internal (register/adder) partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above (AW bits)\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  psum_south  // partial sum out to below (AW bits)\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   reg [PW-1:0] ps;\t// narrow accumulator register\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [PW-1:0] prod_x = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps_in  = psum_north[PW-1:0]; // already sign-extended upstream\n   wire [PW-1:0] nxt;\n   ksadd #(.W(PW)) u_add (.A(ps_in), .B(prod_x), .S(nxt));\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         ps     <= nxt;\n      end\n   end\n\n   // present full-width, sign-extended value downward (pure wiring)\n   assign psum_south = {{(AW-PW){ps[PW-1]}}, ps};\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19)\t // inter-row partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column (AW bits)\n    );\n\n   // per-row internal width (non-decreasing going south)\n   function integer pwf;\n      input integer i;\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            0:      pwf = 16;\n            1:      pwf = 17;\n            2:      pwf = 17;\n            3,4,5,6: pwf = 18;\n            default: pwf = 19;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW), .PW(pwf(i))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // output partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with tapered partial sums, AW-bit output.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]

  -> edit_file(filename='design_v6.v', old_string="   wire [6*W-1:0] G, P;\n   assign G[W-1:0] = A & B;\n   assign P[W-1:0] = A ^ B;\n   genvar l, k;\n   generate\n      for (l = 1; l <= 5; l = l + 1) begin : lvl\n         for (k = 0; k < W; k = k + 1) begin : b\n            if (k >= (1 << l)) begin : merge\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k] |\n                                 (P[(l-1)*W+k] & G[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<l)]);\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k] & P[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<l)];\n            end\n            else begin : pass\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k];\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign S = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[5*W + W-2 : 5*W], 1'b0};", new_string="   wire [6*W-1:0] G, P;\n   assign G[W-1:0] = A & B;\n   assign P[W-1:0] = A ^ B;\n   genvar l, k;\n   generate\n      for (l = 1; l <= 5; l = l + 1) begin : lvl\n         // Kogge-Stone offset at level l is 2^(l-1): 1,2,4,8,16\n         for (k = 0; k < W; k = k + 1) begin : b\n            if (k >= (1 << (l-1))) begin : merge\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k] |\n                                 (P[(l-1)*W+k] & G[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<(l-1))]);\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k] & P[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<(l-1))];\n            end\n            else begin : pass\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k];\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign S = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[5*W + W-2 : 5*W], 1'b0};")

  -> create_file(filename='design_v7.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v7:\n//  Sign-magnitude PEs + tapered internal widths (v6 width table) + KS adders.\n//\n//  PE structure:\n//   - weight stored as magnitude wm (8b) + sign ws, precomputed on load.\n//   - activation magnitude am computed from registered activation each cycle.\n//   - product magnitude mag = am * wm (unsigned 8x8), sign s = as ^ ws.\n//   - accumulator: new_psum = psum + prod, with prod = s ? ~mag+1 : mag.\n//     Split: lo = psum[15:0] + (s? ~mag:mag) + s   (16-bit add)\n//            hi = psum[PW-1:17] ^ s                (plain XOR gates)\n//     This is exact because bit 15 of any intermediate psum is 0\n//     (|psum| <= 2^(PW-1) <= 2^17 => bit15 = value>>15 < 4, and after a\n//     valid MAC the magnitude bound keeps bit 15 clear), so no carry ever\n//     leaves the 16-bit adder; bits >= 17 flip iff s=1, and the MSB equals\n//     lo-cout ^ s.\n//   - psum register is only PW bits (tapered per row).\n//\n//  Critical path = w-flipflop -> unsigned mult -> 16-bit add -> sign XOR,\n//  versus v6's signed mult -> PW-bit KS adder.\n\nmodule ksadd #(parameter W = 16) (\n    input  [W-1:0] A,\n    input  [W-1:0] B,\n    output [W-1:0] S\n);\n   // Kogge-Stone with offsets 1,2,4,...,2^(L-1) where L = clog2(W)\n   localparam L = 4; // W = 16\n   wire [(L+1)*W-1:0] G, P;\n   assign G[W-1:0] = A & B;\n   assign P[W-1:0] = A ^ B;\n   genvar l, k;\n   generate\n      for (l = 1; l <= L; l = l + 1) begin : lvl\n         for (k = 0; k < W; k = k + 1) begin : b\n            if (k >= (1 << (l-1))) begin : merge\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k] |\n                                 (P[(l-1)*W+k] & G[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<(l-1))]);\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k] & P[(l-1)*W+k-(1<<(l-1))];\n            end\n            else begin : pass\n               assign G[l*W+k] = G[(l-1)*W+k];\n               assign P[l*W+k] = P[(l-1)*W+k];\n            end\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n   assign S = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[L*W + W-2 : L*W], 1'b0};\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19,\t // inter-row (port) partial-sum width\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // internal (register/adder) partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above (2's comp)\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above (AW bits)\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below (2's comp)\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  psum_south  // partial sum out to below (AW bits)\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight, stored sign-magnitude (precomputed at load)\n   reg [DW-1:0] wm;\t// magnitude\n   reg\t       ws;\t// sign\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_mag_in = w_north[DW-1] ? (~w_north + 1'b1) : w_north;\n   assign w_south = ws ? (~wm + 1'b1) : wm; // back to two's complement\n\n   // activation magnitude\n   reg [DW-1:0] a;\t// two's complement activation register\n   assign a_east = a;\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_mag = a[DW-1] ? (~a + 1'b1) : a;\n   wire\t       as = a[DW-1];\n\n   // product magnitude + sign\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] mag = a_mag * wm;\n   wire\t       s = as ^ ws;\n\n   // accumulation\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps_in = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [15:0] lo_in  = ps_in[15:0];\n   wire [15:0] mag_n  = s ? ~mag[15:0] : mag[15:0];\n   wire [15:0] lo;\n   ksadd #(.W(16)) u_add (.A(lo_in), .B(mag_n), .S(lo));\n   // adder carry-in s folds into ksadd by adding (mag_n + s) instead:\n   //   lo_in + mag_n + s ; emulate with B = mag_n ^ {16{s}} then cin=s below.\n   // ksadd has no cin port; compute B = s ? ~mag : mag and add s via + on A?\n   // Simpler: let B = mag_n, A = lo_in + s (16-bit increment), then ksadd.\n\n   // final PW-bit next value: {ps_hi ^ s, lo_cout ^ s, lo[15:0]}\n   wire lo_cout_xor; // = MSB of next = ps_in[PW-1] ^ s ... see below\n\n   // high part (bits PW-1 down to 17), flipped when s\n   wire [PW-17:1] hi_nxt = ps_in[PW-1:17] ^ {(PW-17){s}};\n   // bit 16 = sign of the new psum = ps_in[PW-1] ^ s (since bit15 never carries)\n   wire sign_nxt = ps_in[PW-1] ^ s;\n   wire [PW-1:0] nxt = {hi_nxt, sign_nxt, lo[14:0], (ps_in[15] ^ s)};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         wm <= w_mag_in;\n         ws <= w_north[DW-1];\n      end\n      if (en)\n        a <= a_west;\n   end\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (en)\n        ps_store <= nxt;\n   end\n   reg [PW-1:0] ps_store;\n\n   assign psum_south = {{(AW-PW){ps_store[PW-1]}}, ps_store};\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19)\t // inter-row partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column (AW bits)\n    );\n\n   // per-row internal width (non-decreasing going south)\n   function integer pwf;\n      input integer i;\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            0:      pwf = 16;\n            1:      pwf = 17;\n            2:      pwf = 17;\n            3,4,5,6: pwf = 18;\n            default: pwf = 19;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW), .PW(pwf(i))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // output partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with tapered partial sums, AW-bit output.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")
