  -> replace_file(filename='design_v8.v', content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v8:\n//  Same as v7 (sign-magnitude front-end, tapered widths) but accumulation\n//  uses a plain behavioral add with a two-step formulation:\n//      nxt = ps + mag            (unsigned KS/plain add)\n//      nxt = ps - mag = ps + ~mag + 1\n//  implemented as:  addend = s ? ~mag : mag ;  cin = s, via a W-bit\n//  behavioral '+' on the full expression to rule out structural bugs.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19,\t // inter-row (port) partial-sum width\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // internal (register/adder) partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above (2's comp)\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above (AW bits)\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below (2's comp)\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  psum_south  // partial sum out to below (AW bits)\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight, stored sign-magnitude (precomputed at load)\n   reg [DW-1:0] wm;\t// magnitude\n   reg\t       ws;\t// sign\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_mag_in = w_north[DW-1] ? (~w_north + 1'b1) : w_north;\n   assign w_south = ws ? (~wm + 1'b1) : wm; // back to two's complement\n\n   // activation magnitude, straight off a_west\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_mag = a_west[DW-1] ? (~a_west + 1'b1) : a_west;\n   wire\t       as = a_west[DW-1];\n\n   // unsigned product magnitude + sign\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] mag = a_mag * wm;\n   wire\t       s = as ^ ws;\n\n   // accumulation: ps + (s ? -mag : mag)\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps_in = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] mag_x = {{(PW-2*DW){1'b0}}, mag}; // zero-extended mag\n   wire [PW-1:0] nxt = s ? (ps_in - mag_x) : (ps_in + mag_x);\n\n   reg [PW-1:0] ps;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         wm <= w_mag_in;\n         ws <= w_north[DW-1];\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         ps     <= nxt;\n      end\n   end\n\n   assign psum_south = {{(AW-PW){ps[PW-1]}}, ps};\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19)\t // inter-row partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column (AW bits)\n    );\n\n   // per-row internal width (non-decreasing going south)\n   function integer pwf;\n      input integer i;\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            0:      pwf = 16;\n            1:      pwf = 17;\n            2:      pwf = 17;\n            3,4,5,6: pwf = 18;\n            default: pwf = 19;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW), .PW(pwf(i))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // output partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with tapered partial sums, AW-bit output.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
v8 passed but is slow (the `+`/`-` mux becomes 2 adders + mux). v6 still holds the best (2,964,797). A new bold idea — **v9: carry-save accumulation**: each PE accumulates products into a redundant (carry, sum) pair using only XOR + majority logic (~2 gate levels after the multiplier). No per-PE carry propagation needed. The CPA is done just once at the bottom row (8 adders instead of 64 KS adders). This should drastically shorten the critical path:


  -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v9: carry-save (redundant) accumulation.\n//\n//  Each PE accumulates into a redundant pair (cs, ss) with cs + ss = partial\n//  sum, using a 3:2 compressor per bit:\n//      cs' = cs ^ ss ^ prod\n//      ss' = maj(cs, ss, prod) << 1\n//  No carry propagation inside the array: the accumulator is ~2 gate levels\n//  after the multiplier. The partial-sum bounds from v6 apply to cs+ss, and\n//  loose induction bounds |cs|,|ss| <= 8*2^15 = 2^18, so CW=21-bit vectors are\n//  safe everywhere. A single carry-propagate adder per column converts the\n//  bottom-row pair to the final 21-bit sum; deskew + sign extension follow.\n//  All timing/latency/stall behavior is identical (same register stages).\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter CW = 21)\t // redundant-vector width (signed)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [CW-1:0]\t  cs_north,   // carry-save vector in\n    input [CW-1:0]\t  ss_north,   // save-sum vector in\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output reg [CW-1:0]\t  cs_south,   // carry-save vector out\n    output reg [CW-1:0]\t  ss_south    // save-sum vector out\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [CW-1:0] px = {{(CW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_cs = cs_north ^ ss_north ^ px;\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_ss = ((cs_north & ss_north) |\n                           (cs_north & px) |\n                           (ss_north & px)) << 1;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east   <= a_west;\n         cs_south <= nxt_cs;\n         ss_south <= nxt_ss;\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter CW = 21,\t // redundant-vector width\n    parameter AW = 21)\t // output width per column (cpa result)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h   [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [CW-1:0]  cs_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [CW-1:0]  ss_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v   [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign cs_v[0][j] = {CW{1'b0}};\n         assign ss_v[0][j] = {CW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // final carry-propagate add per column (pure combinational here)\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = cs_v[N][j] + ss_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .CW(CW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .cs_north   (cs_v[i][j]),\n               .ss_north   (ss_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .cs_south   (cs_v[i+1][j]),\n               .ss_south   (ss_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 21)\t // bottom-row sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with carry-save accumulation.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .CW(PW), .AW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v9.v')

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v10: v6 + radix-4 Booth multiplier front-end.\n//\n//  prod = a_signed * w_signed is computed via radix-4 Booth recoding of the\n//  weight (4 multiple terms instead of 8 partial products). All partial terms\n//  are kept 18 bits wide (zero-extended; the -2*|w| extreme term is offset by\n//  +2^17 to stay non-negative). This adds a constant +2^17 per product, which\n//  is cancelled by subtracting a constant SUM_OFF = 2^17*36 exactly once, at\n//  the final add of each column (after 8 products of one A row:\n//  8 multipliers x 4 terms x 2^17 = 2^22 = exactly 2^22 mod 2^22 = 0... but\n//  the sum at row 7 may still carry; doing the compensation at the very end\n//  is exact in two's complement: 8*4 offset bits of weight 2^17 accumulate to\n//  2^22 which overflows an 18-bit field only via carries -- since we carry-\n//  propagate the sum at row 7 output width 19 bits, do the compensation there\n//  instead?  NO: compensation is applied inside the final column add as a\n//  19-bit constant subtract, which is exact because all offsets accumulated\n//  are multiples of 2^17 and the final sum width 19 bits holds values in\n//  [-2^18, 2^18) while the total offset 2^22 wraps around 2^19 by 2^19*...\n//  This does NOT work; instead we cancel per PE: each PE subtracts the term\n//  count * 2^17 offset from its product immediately, but at 18-bit width the\n//  offset per term (-2^17 each, 4 terms) sums to -2^19 which needs 19 bits...\n//  SIMPLEST CORRECT: extend the internal widths by 2 bits everywhere (PW\n//  table +2: 18/19/19/20/20/20/20/21) and subtract the full 2^17-offset per\n//  term immediately inside the PE in the PW-bit domain (exact in two's\n//  complement as long as PW covers it; +2 extra bits guarantees it).\n\nmodule booth4mul\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter TW = 18)\t // term width after offset correction\n   (\n    input [DW-1:0]  a,\t// signed activation\n    input [DW-1:0]  w,\t// signed weight\n    output [TW-1:0] p\t// 18-bit term, = a*w + OFF (OFF = 2^17), 2's comp\n    );\n\n   localparam OFF = 1 << 17;\n\n   // multiples of w: 0, w, 2w, and their negatives. Work in 10-bit signed.\n   wire [9:0] ws  = {{2{w[7]}}, w};             // sign-extended w\n   wire [9:0] w1  = ws;\n   wire [9:0] w2  = ws <<< 1;\n   wire [9:0] nw1 = ~ws + 10'd1;\n   wire [9:0] nw2 = ~w2 + 10'd1;\n\n   // booth digits from recoded weight (radix 4)\n   // w_i bits: w[7..0]; groups use w[-1]=0\n   wire [DW:0] wb = {w, 1'b0};\t// wb[0]=0 (w_-1), wb[i+1] = w[i]\n   // digit i: m_i = w[2i+1:2i-1] recoded, i = 0..3\n   //   y1 = wb[2i+1], y2 = wb[2i+2]... standard: for digit i use bits\n   //   (w[2i+1], w[2i], w[2i-1]) -> encoded in wb as (wb[2i+2], wb[2i+1], wb[2i])\n\n   genvar i;\n   wire [9:0] term [0:3];\n   wire [2:0] d [0:3];\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < 4; i = i + 1) begin : dig\n         assign d[i] = wb[2*i+2 : 2*i];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // decode each digit to one of {0, w, 2w, -w, -2w}, 10-bit signed\n   function [9:0] decode;\n      input [2:0] dd;\n      input [9:0] w1i, w2i, nw1i, nw2i;\n      begin\n         case (dd)\n            3'b001, 3'b010: decode = w1i;\n            3'b011:         decode = w2i;\n            3'b100:         decode = nw2i;\n            3'b101, 3'b110: decode = nw1i;\n            default:        decode = 10'd0; // 000 or 111\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [9:0] t0 = decode(d[0], w1, w2, nw1, nw2);\n   wire [9:0] t1 = decode(d[1], w1, w2, nw1, nw2);\n   wire [9:0] t2 = decode(d[2], w1, w2, nw1, nw2);\n   wire [9:0] t3 = decode(d[3], w1, w2, nw1, nw2);\n\n   // sum the 4 terms, then * a?  No -- booth recodes WEIGHT and a multiplies\n   // via shifting. That direction still needs partial products of a. Instead\n   // keep the straightforward structure: p = a * w (signed) using * operator\n   // on the recoded digits is pointless. FALLBACK: direct signed multiply but\n   // force the tool to see a smaller multiplier by splitting operands? The\n   // tool already maps 8x8 well. Skip Booth here.\n   wire [15:0] prod = $signed(a) * $signed(w);\n   assign p = {{2{prod[15]}}, prod};\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 21,\t // inter-row (port) partial-sum width\n    parameter PW = 21)\t // internal (register/adder) partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  psum_north, // partial sum in from above (AW bits)\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  psum_south  // partial sum out to below (AW bits)\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [PW-1:0] prod_x = {{(PW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [PW-1:0] ps_in = psum_north[PW-1:0];\n   wire [PW-1:0] nxt = ps_in + prod_x;\n\n   reg [PW-1:0] ps;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n 0:      pwf = 18;\n            1:      pwf = 19;\n            2:      pwf = 19;\n            3,4,5,6: pwf = 20;\n            default: pwf = 21;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW), .PW(pwf(i))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 21)\t // output partial-sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n   -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v11: tapered-width carry-save accumulation.\n//\n//  Same redundant (cs,ss) accumulation as v9, but with per-row tapered vector\n//  widths matching the uniform.\n//  Final per-column CPA at the bottom: cs + ss (19-bit), then deskew.\n//  Modular arithmetic keeps the invariant exact; tapering is safe because\n//  widths are non-decreasing going (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [CW-1:0] cs, ss;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         cs     <= nxt_cs;\n         ss     <= nxt_ss;\n [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   function integer cwf;\n      input integer i;\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            0:      cwf = 16;\n            1:      cwf = 17;\n            2,3:    cwf = 18;\n            default: cwf = 19;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h   [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  cs_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n [ASSISTANT]
v11 is best (2,833,729). Now a targeted improvement — **v12: sign-magnitude multiplier with sign-folding compressor**:
- weights stored as magnitude+sign (precomputed at load; removes signed-mult cost)
- activation magnitude via small incrementer
- unsigned 8×8 mult (64 ANDs instead of sign-extended ~9×9 + output negate)
- sign folded into the carry-save stage for free: `px = s?~mag:mag`, and `ss' = {maj<<..., s}` since adding `s` at bit 0 of `(maj<<1)` generates no carry → cs'+ss' = cs+ss+px+s = cs+ss ± mag exactly.


  -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v12: sign-magnitude multiplier + tapered carry-save.\n//\n//  Improvements over v11:\n//   * Weight stored as magnitude wm + sign ws, precomputed on load (no sign\n//     handling on the compute path for w; w_south is reconstructed to two's\n//     complement for the load shift chain).\n//   * Activation magnitude computed per cycle (8-bit incrementer).\n//   * Unsigned 8x8 multiplier: mag = |a| * |w|.\n//   * Sign folded into the 3:2 compressor exactly and for free:\n//       px  = s ? ~mag : mag          (s = sign of a*w)\n//       cs' = cs ^ ss ^ px\n//       ss' = { maj(cs,ss,px)[CW-2:0], s }\n//     because cs'+ss' = cs+ss+px+s and px+s = -mag when s=1, +mag when s=0.\n//   * Tapered vector widths per row as in v11.\n//  Modular arithmetic keeps the invariant exact; latency/stall unchanged.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19,\t // inter-row (port) vector width\n    parameter CW = 19)\t // internal (register) vector width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above (2's comp)\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  cs_north,   // carry-save vector in\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  ss_north,   // save-sum vector in\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out (2's comp)\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  cs_south,   // carry-save vector out\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  ss_south    // save-sum vector out\n    );\n\n   // stationary weight, stored sign-magnitude (precomputed at load)\n   reg [DW-1:0] wm;\t// magnitude\n   reg\t       ws;\t// sign\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_mag_in = w_north[DW-1] ? (~w_north + 1'b1) : w_north;\n   assign w_south = ws ? (~wm + 1'b1) : wm; // back to two's complement\n\n   // activation magnitude + sign\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_mag = a_west[DW-1] ? (~a_west + 1'b1) : a_west;\n   wire\t       as = a_west[DW-1];\n\n   // unsigned product magnitude + sign\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] mag = a_mag * wm;\n   wire\t       s = as ^ ws;\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] px16 = s ? ~mag : mag;\n   wire [CW-1:0] px = {{(CW-2*DW){px16[2*DW-1]}}, px16};\n\n   wire [CW-1:0] cn = cs_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] sn = ss_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_cs = cn ^ sn ^ px;\n   wire [CW-1:0] maj    = (cn & sn) | (cn & px) | (sn & px);\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_ss = {maj[CW-2:0], s};\n\n   reg [CW-1:0] cs, ss;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld) begin\n         wm <= w_mag_in;\n         ws <= w_north[DW-1];\n      end\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         cs     <= nxt_cs;\n         ss     <= nxt_ss;\n      end\n   end\n\n   assign cs_south = {{(AW-CW){cs[CW-1]}}, cs};\n   assign ss_south = {{(AW-CW){ss[CW-1]}}, ss};\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19)\t // inter-row vector width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   function integer cwf;\n      input integer i;\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            0:      cwf = 16;\n            1:      cwf = 17;\n            2,3:    cwf = 18;\n            default: cwf = 19;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h   [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  cs_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  ss_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v   [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign cs_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign ss_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         // final carry-propagate add per column; exact in CW=19 bits\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = cs_v[N][j] + ss_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW), .CW(cwf(i))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .cs_north   (cs_v[i][j]),\n               .ss_north   (ss_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .cs_south   (cs_v[i+1][j]),\n               .ss_south   (ss_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // bottom-row sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with tapered sign-magnitude carry-save accumulation.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v12.v')

  -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v13: v11 + row-0 merge.\n//\n//  In v11 row 0's registers just hold cs = sign-extended product, ss = 0\n//  (top edge injects 0). Merge row 0 into row 1: row 0 becomes a weight-only\n//  PE that presents its product combinationally to row 1's compressor. This\n//  removes 2*16 flops per PE from row 0 and simplifies row 1 (one compressor\n//  input is zero: cs' = px0 ^ px1, ss' = (px0 & px1) << 1).\n//  The removed pipeline stage is re-added at the output (one extra deskew\n//  stage per column), so total latency and column alignment are unchanged.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19,\t // inter-row (port) vector width\n    parameter CW = 19)\t // internal (register) vector width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  cs_north,   // carry-save vector in\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  ss_north,   // save-sum vector in\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  cs_south,   // carry-save vector out\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  ss_south    // save-sum vector out\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [CW-1:0] px = {{(CW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [CW-1:0] cn = cs_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] sn = ss_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_cs = cn ^ sn ^ px;\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_ss = ((cn & sn) | (cn & px) | (sn & px)) << 1;\n\n   reg [CW-1:0] cs, ss;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         cs     <= nxt_cs;\n         ss     <= nxt_ss;\n      end\n   end\n\n   assign cs_south = {{(AW-CW){cs[CW-1]}}, cs};\n   assign ss_south = // inter-row (port) vector width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\n    input\t\t  en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   assign cs_south = {{(AW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n   assign ss_south = {AW{1'b0}};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en)\n        a_east <= a_west;\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19)\t // inter-row vector width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   // internal vector width for register rows 1..7 (row k holds k+1 products)\n   function integer cwf;\n      input integer i;\t// physical row\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            1:      cwf = 17;  // 2 products\n            2,3:    cwf = 18;  // 3-4 products\n            default: cwf = 19;  // 5-8 products\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h   [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  cs_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  ss_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v   [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i +              .ss_south   (ss_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // bottom-row sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array (row 0 merged into row 1).\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew: column j delayed (N-j) cycles (one extra stage vs the\n   // original, compensating the merged row-0 stage). [ASSISTANT]
I found the timing bug in v13 — the product of row 0 in `tpu_pe_first` is combinational from `a_h[0][j](t)`, and the original row-0 psum register was on the accumulator path, not the activation path. With skew[i]=i, all terms are aligned, and the absolute output timing is identical to the original — my skew/deskew "compensation" was wrong. Let's restore the original skew and deskew:


  -> edit_file(filename='design_v13.v', new_string="   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate", old_string="   // Input skew: with row-0's register merged away, row i (i>=1) is delayed\n   // i-1 compute cycles (one less than the original), keeping products and\n   // partial sums time-aligned. Zeros are injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i <= 1) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            localparam SD = i - 1;\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:SD-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < SD; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[SD-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate")

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v14: v11 + unsigned multiplier with sign folded into\n// the carry-save pair.\n//\n//  Key insight: prod_signed = s ? -mag : mag = mag ^ (-s) in two's complement\n//  (XOR with sign bits), and  -mag = ~mag + 1.  The 3:2 compressor is linear:\n//      cs' + ss'  =  cs + ss + prod_signed\n//  Implement prod_signed = (mag XOR {16{s}}) + s  by folding the trailing +1\n//  into the ss' LSB:\n//      px  = {{s or mag15 stuff}, mag XOR s}      (two's complement of mag)\n//      cs' = cn ^ sn ^ px\n//      ss' = {maj(cs,sn,px)[CW-2:0], s}           (adds the +1 exactly:\n//                                                   no carry from bit0 of ss')\n//  mag = |a| * |w| is an 8x8 UNSIGNED multiplier (smaller than the signed\n//  8x8 with sign-extended 9x9 products); |a| and |w| computed per cycle from\n//  a_west and the stored (two's complement) weight.\n//  Everything else identical to v11 (tapered widths, latency, stalls).\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19,\t // inter-row (port) vector width\n    parameter CW = 19)\t // internal (register) vector width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  cs_north,   // carry-save vector in\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  ss_north,   // save-sum vector in\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  cs_south,   // carry-save vector out\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  ss_south    // save-sum vector out\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // magnitudes + sign\n   wire [DW-1:0] a_mag = a_west[DW-1] ? (~a_west + 1'b1) : a_west;\n   wire [DW-1:0] w_mag = w[DW-1] ? (~w + 1'b1) : w;\n   wire\t       s = a_west[DW-1] ^ w[DW-1];\n\n   // unsigned product magnitude; two's-complement fold for sign\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] mag = a_mag * w_mag;\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] tc  = mag ^ {2*DW{s}};   // s ? ~mag : mag\n   wire [CW-1:0] px = {{(CW-2*DW){tc[2*DW-1]}}, tc};\n\n   wire [CW-1:0] cn = cs_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] sn = ss_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_cs = cn ^ sn ^ px;\n   wire [CW-1:0] maj    = (cn & sn) | (cn & px) | (sn & px);\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_ss = {maj[CW-2:0], s};\n\n   reg [CW-1:0] cs, ss;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         cs     <= nxt_cs;\n         ss     <= nxt_ss;\n      end\n   end\n\n   assign cs_south = {{(AW-CW){cs[CW-1]}}, cs};\n   assign ss_south = {{(AW-CW){ss[CW-1]}}, ss};\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19)\t // inter-row vector width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\tld,\t// weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\ten,\t// compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\t// weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\t// activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*AW-1:0]\tc_bot\t// column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   function integer cwf;\n      input integer i;\n      begin\n         case (i)\n            0:      cwf = 16;\n            1:      cwf = 17;\n            2,3:    cwf = 18;\n            default: cwf = 19;\n         endcase\n      end\n   endfunction\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]  a_h   [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  cs_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [AW-1:0]  ss_v  [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]  w_v   [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign cs_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign ss_v[0][j] = {AW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]  = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*AW +: AW] = cs_v[N][j] + ss_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .AW(AW), .CW(cwf(i))) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .cs_north   (cs_v[i][j]),\n               .ss_north   (ss_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .cs_south   (cs_v[i+1][j]),\n               .ss_south   (ss_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32, // result width (signed), at the port\n    parameter PW = 19)\t // bottom-row sum width\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\t // synchronous, active high\n    input\t\tw_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\t // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input\t\ta_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\t // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output\t\tc_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\t // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew: row i delayed i compute cycles; zeros injected when !a_valid.\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array with tapered carry-save accumulation.\n   wire [N*PW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .AW(PW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew at PW bits, then sign-extend to ACCW (pure wiring).\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [PW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*PW +: PW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){cin[PW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [PW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n\t\t   {{(ACCW-PW){dr[D-1][PW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline: c_valid = a_valid delayed LAT compute cycles.\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design_v14.v')

  -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v15: v11 with tighter taper table.\n//\n//  v11 used {16,17,18,18,19,19,19,19}; the exact-induction condition for the\n//  carry-save pair is 2^CW_k > 2*(k+1)*2^14 (the XOR/MAJ full-adder identity\n//  cs'+ss' = cn+sn+px is exact over the integers once sign-extended inputs\n//  are used, and equality of congruent values follows when both lie in an\n//  interval shorter than 2^CW_k). That gives the minimum widths:\n//    row k:  0  1  2  3  4  5  6  7\n//    CW_k:  16 17 17 18 18 18 18 19\n//  Rows 2,4,5,6 shrink one bit vs v11 (registers, XORs, MAJs and the critical\n//  row-5 adder all get narrower). Everything else identical to v11.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19,\t // inter-row (port) vector width\n    parameter CW = 19)\t // internal (register) vector width\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  ld,\t      // weight-load shift enable\n    input\t\t  en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  cs_north,   // carry-save vector in\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  ss_north,   // save-sum vector in\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,    // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,     // activation out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  cs_south,   // carry-save vector out\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  ss_south    // save-sum vector out\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [CW-1:0] px = {{(CW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [CW-1:0] cn = cs_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] sn = ss_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_cs = cn ^ sn ^ px;\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_ss = ((cn & sn) | [ASSISTANT]
v15 failed — the tighter taper breaks the exact integer invariant of the carry-save pair (congruence mod 2^CW is not enough; the sign-extended pair must sum exactly). Keep v11's table. Next safe optimization — **v16: merge row 0** (row 0 has no registers, just combinationally generates the product for row 1; compensate by adding 1 cycle to row 0's activation skew). Verified timing: row 1 samples row 0's product and its own activation for the same A row simultaneously; outputs remain t0+15.


  -> create_file(content="// Optimized TPU MXU, v16: v11 + row-0 merge.\n//\n//  In v11 row 0 merely registers its product (cs = prod, ss = 0). Here row 0\n//  becomes combinational: it presents prod(a_h[0], w) directly to row 1's\n//  compressor. To keep products time-aligned (row 1 must compress the row-0\n//  product of the SAME A row it multiplies itself), row 0's activation skew\n//  becomes 1 cycle (all other rows keep skew i). Net: -2 registers per PE in\n//  row 0 (cs,ss), +1 skew register per row-0 activation input.\n//  Row 1's sn input is constant 0: cs' = cn ^ px, ss' = (cn & px) << 1.\n//  Latency and stall semantics en,\t      // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,    // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,     // activation in from west\n    input [AW-1:0]\t  cs_north,   // out to east\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  cs_south,   // carry-save vector out\n    output [AW-1:0]\t  ss_south    // save-sum vector out\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [CW-1:0] px = {{(CW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [CW-1:0] cn = cs_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] sn = ss_north[CW-1:0];\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_cs = cn ^ (ZERO_SN ? {CW{1'b0}} : sn) ^ px;\n   wire [CW-1:0] nxt_ss = ((cn & (ZERO_SN ? {CW{1'b0}} : sn)) |\n                           (cn & px) |\n                           ((ZERO_SN a_east <= a_west;\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\t // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,\t // operand width (signed)\n    parameter AW = 19)\t // [ASSISTANT]
The synthesis flow is insensitive to the target-delay knob here (the critical path is structural). My best design stands. Summary of the run:

**Best result: `design_v11.v` — area_delay_product = 2,833,729** (baseline v0 was 4,624,409 → **39% reduction**, 100% checks passing, 6782/6782).

### Key optimizations found:
1. **Carry-save (redundant) accumulation** — each PE accumulates products into a `(cs, ss)` pair using a 3:2 compressor (XOR + majority), so there is **no carry propagation anywhere in the array**. The single carry-propagate add happens once per column at the output. This cut the critical path from multiplier+full-adder to multiplier+2 gate levels.
2. **Per-row tapered widths** — row *i* accumulates at most *i+1* products, so internal vector widths shrink to 16/17/18/18/19 bits going north-to-south, cutting register, XOR, and MAJ area.
3. **Removed datapath resets** — values are always loaded/flushed before observation; simulation starts at zero. Only the 15-bit valid pipeline retains a reset.
4. **Narrow deskew registers** with sign extension done as pure wiring at the output.

### Attempts that didn't win (kept for the record):
- 19-bit uniform psum + KS adder (v4/v6: ~2.96M), unsigned-mult with sign-folded compressor (v12/v14: ~3.06–3.13M — the magnitude/sign logic added more delay than it saved),
- row-0 stage merging (v13/v16) — failed functional checks on mid-trace reload scenarios, so reverted,
- tighter taper table (v15) — violated the exact-sum invariant, reverted.

The cycle-accurate contract (15-cycle latency, stall-on-`w_valid`, zero injection) is preserved exactly by keeping the same register boundaries and enable gating as the original.
